2026-07-16 12:33:14.312 +05:30 [INF] Executed DbCommand (209ms) [Parameters=[@p1='100', @p='0'], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='120']
SELECT p0."Id", p0."Name", p0."BrandId", b0."Name" AS "BrandName", p0."SKU", p0."IsActive", p0."CurrentStock", p0."CreatedAt", p0."UpdatedAt"
FROM (
    SELECT p."Id", p."BrandId", p."CreatedAt", p."CurrentStock", p."IsActive", p."Name", p."SKU", p."UpdatedAt"
    FROM aquora_tenant_test_company_2."Products" AS p
    WHERE NOT (p."IsDeleted")
    ORDER BY p."CreatedAt" DESC
    LIMIT @p1 OFFSET @p
) AS p0
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT b."Id", b."Name"
    FROM aquora_tenant_test_company_2."Brands" AS b
    WHERE NOT (b."IsDeleted")
) AS b0 ON p0."BrandId" = b0."Id"
ORDER BY p0."CreatedAt" DESC
